\underline{v}\in C^{2}(\overline{J};\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(\Omega)\times H^{1+\nu_{v}}(\Omega))
with
\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},\nu_{v}\in(\frac{1}{2},k+1]
. Let
r:=\max(\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}-1,0)
if
k^{\prime}\geq 1
and
r:=\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}-1+s\geq 0
if
k^{\prime}=0
. Assume that
\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}\in C^{0}(\overline{J};H^{r}(\Omega))
(this follows from
\boldsymbol{\sigma}\in C^{1}(\overline{J};\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(\Omega))
whenever
k^{\prime}\geq 1
and
\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\geq 1
). Then we have

\displaystyle\lVert\utilde{e}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(T_{f})\rVert_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\lesssim h^{s}\ell_{\Omega}^{-s}T_{f}\Big\{h^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}|\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{C^{2}(\overline{J};\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(\Omega))}+\ell_{\Omega}h^{\nu_{v}}|v|_{C^{2}(\overline{J};H^{1+\nu_{v}}(\Omega))}

\displaystyle+h^{1+r-\delta}\ell_{\Omega}^{\delta}|\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{C^{0}(\overline{J};H^{r}(\Omega))}\Big\}+h\big\{h^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{0}|_{\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(\Omega)}+\ell_{\Omega}h^{\nu_{v}}|v_{0}|_{H^{1+\nu_{v}}(\Omega)}\},

where the bound on the last term involving the initial condition follows from (
3.1a
). Notice that
1+r-\delta\geq\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}
for
k^{\prime}\geq 1
and
1+r-\delta=\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}
for
k^{\prime}=0
. Thus, in all cases for
k^{\prime}
, the term involving
|\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{C^{0}(\overline{J};H^{r}(\Omega))}
converges at least at the same rate as the other two terms between braces.
Altogether, when
\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}=\nu_{v}=k+1
and
s=1
, the above estimate converges at rate
\mathcal{O}(h^{k+2})
.

Remark 3.18

(
k^{\prime}=0
)
.

The modification in the analysis for
k^{\prime}=0
whereby some slight additional
regularity on
\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}
is required, is inspired from the
L^{2}
-error analysis
of HHO methods in the elliptic case (see
[
4
, Lem. 2.11]
). This
is the first time this idea is used in the time-dependent context.

Remark 3.19

(Time-integrated primal variable)
.

In the context of the second-order formulation in time of the wave equation, the time-integrated primal variable has a physical meaning. For instance, it can be intepreted as a displacement when the primal variable represents a velocity. In this case, Theorem
3.16
provides an improved
L^{2}
-error bound on the displacement. To evaluate this error, it can be more computationally effective, as in
[
8
]
, to evolve in time only the cell mean-values of the discrete velocity and use the discrete gradient at final time.

4
Preliminary results

In this section, we state and prove the auxiliary
results that we need to prove our main results. We gather them in three groups. The first is about general polynomials. The second is about standard HHO results, and the third is about some novel results on the HHO stabilization.

In what follows, the inequality
a\leq Cb
for positive numbers
a
and
b
is often abbreviated as
a\lesssim b
. The value of
C
can be different at each occurence provided it is independent of the parameters
\ell_{\Omega}
,
T_{f},
and the mesh-size
h
; the value can depend on the mesh shape-regularity, the polynomial degree, and the space dimension. We also write
a\approx b
whenever
a\lesssim b\lesssim a
.

4.1
Two results on polynomials

Lemma 4.1

(Bound on vector-valued polynomials)
.

For all
T\in\mathcal{T}
, fixing any
d
faces
\{F_{i}\}_{i=1,\dots,d}\subset\mathcal{F}_{\partial T}
, the following holds: For all
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(T;\mathbb{R}^{d})
,

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{q}\rVert_{T}\lesssim\lVert\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{T}^{k-1}(\boldsymbol{q})\rVert_{T}+\sum_{i\in\{1:d\}}h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{q}{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{F_{i}},

with the convention that
\mathbb{P}^{-1}(T;\mathbb{R}^{d})=\{\mathbb{0}\}
.

Proof.

A scaling argument and
[
15
, Lemma 2.1]
provide the expected estimate.
∎

For all
T\in\mathcal{T}
, we define the space

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})

\displaystyle:=\{q\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}(T;\mathbb{R}):(q,r)_{T}=0\quad\forall r\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})\},

(4.1)

with the convention that
\mathbb{P}^{-1}(T;\mathbb{R})=\{0\}
so that the condition in the above definition is trivial for
k=0
.

Lemma 4.2

(Bound on scalar-valued polynomials)
.

For all
T\in\mathcal{T}
, the following holds: For all
p\in\mathbb{P}_{\perp}^{k^{\prime}}(T;\mathbb{R})
,

2
h_T^

1

2

∥
p
∥
_
∂
T
for
k’=k,

h_T
∥∇
p
∥
_T +h_T^

1

2

∥
p
∥
_
∂
T
for
k’=k+1.

Proof.

For
k^{\prime}=k
, the proof is given in
[
7
, Lemma A.1]
. Here, we prove the estimate for
k^{\prime}=k+1
. Let
T\in\mathcal{T}
and
p\in\mathbb{P}_{\perp}^{k+1}(T;\mathbb{R})
.
Notice that
(\Pi^{k}_{T}(p),r)_{T}=(p,r)_{T}=0
for all
r\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})
. Invoking (
4.2
) for
\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)
, we infer that
\lVert\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}\lesssim h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{\partial T}
. This, the triangle inequality, and the discrete trace inequality lead to

\displaystyle\lVert p\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert p-\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}+\lVert\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert p-\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)-p\rVert_{\partial T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert p\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert p-\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert p\rVert_{\partial T}.

The observation that
\lVert p-\Pi^{k}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}\leq\lVert p-\Pi^{0}_{T}(p)\rVert_{T}\lesssim h_{T}\lVert\nabla p\rVert_{T}
from the
L^{2}
-othogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{T}
and the Poincaré inequality conclude the proof.
∎

4.2
Basic HHO results

Lemma 4.3

(Stability)
.

For all
\hat{v}_{T}\in\hat{V}_{T}^{k}
and all
T\in\mathcal{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla v_{T}\rVert_{T}^{2}+h_{T}^{-1}\lVert v_{T}-v_{\partial T}\rVert^{2}_{\partial T}\approx\lVert\boldsymbol{G}_{T}(\hat{v}_{T})\rVert_{T}^{2}+h_{T}^{-1}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\hat{v}_{T})\rVert^{2}_{\partial T}.

Proof.

See
[
13
,
14
, Lemma 4]
or
[
4
, Lemma 2.6]
.
∎

Lemma 4.4

(Bound on the stabilization term using
\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}
)
.

For all
v\in H^{1}(T)
and all
T\in\mathcal{T}
, we have

\displaystyle h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}\lesssim\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v))\rVert_{T}.

(4.3)

Consequently, we have, for all
p\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}(T;\mathbb{R})
,

\displaystyle S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(p,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(p|_{\partial T}))=0.

(4.4)

Proof.

For the equal-order case, see
[
14
, Lemma 3]
or
[
4
, Lemma 2.7]
. Let us consider the mixed-order case. It is proved in
[
4
, Lemma 3.4]
that
h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}(\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}\lesssim\lVert\nabla(v-\Pi_{T}^{k+1}(v))\rVert_{T}
. Here, we establish the slightly stronger estimate (
4.3
). The definition of the stabilization operator (
2.9
) and the
L^{2}
-stability of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
lead to

\displaystyle\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}(\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}=\lVert\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}((\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-v)|_{\partial T})\rVert_{\partial T}\leq\lVert\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-v\rVert_{\partial T}.

(4.5)

The triangle inequality, the discrete trace inequality, and the multiplicative trace inequality show that

\displaystyle\lVert\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-v\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)\rVert_{\partial T}+\lVert{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)-v\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)\rVert_{T}+h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)-v\rVert_{T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla({\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)-v)\rVert_{T}.

The observation
\lVert\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)\rVert_{T}\leq\lVert v-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)\rVert_{T}
from the Pythagoras identity, and the Poincaré inequality result in

\displaystyle\lVert\Pi^{k+1}_{T}(v)-v\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)-v\rVert_{T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla({\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)-v)\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla({\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v)-v)\rVert_{T}.

(4.6)

The combination of (
4.5
)-(
4.6
) concludes the proof.
∎

4.3
Novel results on HHO stabilization

Lemma 4.5

(Bound on the stabilization term using
\Pi^{v}_{T}
)
.

For all
T\in\mathcal{T}
and all
\underline{v}:=(\boldsymbol{\sigma},v)\in\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(T)\times H^{1}(T)
with
\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}>\frac{1}{2}
, the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}\lesssim h_{T}^{-1}\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v)\rVert_{T}+\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}^{k+1}_{T}(v))\rVert_{T}.

(4.7)

Proof.

(1) In the equal-order case (
k^{\prime}=k
), the definition (
2.9
) of
S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}
leads to

\displaystyle S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))

\displaystyle=\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}\Big\{\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})|_{\partial T}-\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})+((1-\Pi^{k}_{T})R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})))|_{\partial T}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}\Big\{\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})|_{\partial T}-v|_{\partial T}+((1-\Pi^{k}_{T})R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})))|_{\partial T}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}\Big\{(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k}_{T}(v))|_{\partial T}+(\Pi^{k}_{T}(\eta)-\eta)|_{\partial T}\Big\},

where
\eta:=v-R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))
. The
L^{2}
-stability of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
and the triangle inequality imply that

\displaystyle\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k}_{T}(v)\rVert_{\partial T}+\lVert\eta-\Pi^{k}_{T}(\eta)\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k}_{T}(v)\rVert_{T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla\eta\rVert_{T},

(4.8)

with a discrete trace inequality and the Poincaré inequality in the second step. Notice from the definition of
R_{T}
that, for all
q\in\mathbb{P}^{k+1}_{*}(T;\mathbb{R})
,

\displaystyle(\nabla R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),\nabla q)_{T}

\displaystyle=-(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Delta q)_{T}+(\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}),\nabla q{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=-(v,\Delta q)_{T}+(v,\nabla q{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=(\nabla v,\nabla q)_{T},

where the second equality follows from the definition (
2.18a
) of
\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})
since
\Delta q\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})
and the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
since
\nabla q{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})
, and the third equality follows from an integration by parts. The above identity implies that
\nabla R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))=\nabla{\cal{E}}^{k+1}_{T}(v)
.
This in (
4.8
) gives

\displaystyle h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}\lesssim h_{T}^{-1}\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k}_{T}(v)\rVert_{T}+\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}^{k+1}_{T}(v))\rVert_{T},

(4.9)

which proves (
4.7
).

(2) In the mixed-order case (
k^{\prime}=k+1
), the definition (
2.9
) of
S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}
and the
L^{2}
-stability of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
lead to

\displaystyle\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=\lVert\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}((\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-v)|_{\partial T})\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-v\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi_{T}^{k^{\prime}}(v)\rVert_{\partial T}+\lVert\Pi_{T}^{k^{\prime}}(v)-v\rVert_{\partial T},

(4.10)

by the triangle inequality. The application of a discrete trace inequality for the first term and (
4.6
) for the second term on the right-hand side of (
4.10
) concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 4.6

(Relation between stabilization and divergence of dual variable)
.

For all
T\in\mathcal{T}
and all
\underline{v}:=(\boldsymbol{\sigma},v)\in(\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(T)\cap\boldsymbol{H}(\nabla{\cdot};T))\times H^{\nu_{v}}(T)
with
\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}>\frac{1}{2}
and
\nu_{v}>\frac{1}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\tau_{T}(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}=(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma},w)_{T}\qquad\forall w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R}).

Proof.

Let
w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
. The equation (
2.17c
) with
\mu:=\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})
and the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
lead to

\displaystyle\tau_{T}(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),{}

\displaystyle S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T}))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{\partial T})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}))){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w)_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{\partial T})-\boldsymbol{\sigma}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w)_{\partial T}+((\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}))){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w)_{\partial T}.

The equation (
2.17d
) for the first term and an integration by parts for the second term imply that

\displaystyle\tau_{T}(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),{}

\displaystyle S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})-\boldsymbol{\sigma},\nabla w)_{T}+(\nabla{\cdot}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})),w)_{T}+(\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}),\nabla w)_{T}

\displaystyle=(\nabla{\cdot}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})),w)_{T}

\displaystyle=(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma},w)_{T},

where we used
(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}),w)_{T}=0
since
w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
. This completes the proof.
∎

Recall the polynomial space
\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
from (
4.1
). For any linear form
\Phi:\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})\to\mathbb{R}
, we set
\lVert\Phi\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}:=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})\setminus\{0\}}\frac{\lvert\Phi(w)\rvert}{\lVert w\rVert_{T}}
.

Lemma 4.7

(Bound on polynomials in
\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
by HHO stabilization)
.

For all
T\in\mathcal{T}
and all
p\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert p\rVert_{T}\lesssim h_{T}\lVert b_{p}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}},

where
b_{p}:\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})\to\mathbb{R}
is the linear form defined by

\displaystyle b_{p}(w):=(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(p,0),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}\qquad\forall w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R}).

(4.11)

Proof.

Let
p\in\mathbb{P}_{\perp}^{k^{\prime}}(T;\mathbb{R})
.
Invoking Lemma
4.2
followed by the lower bound from Lemma
4.3
with
\hat{v}_{T}:=(p,0)\in\hat{V}_{T}^{k}
gives

\displaystyle h_{T}^{-1}\lVert p\rVert^{2}_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}\lVert\nabla p\rVert^{2}_{T}+\lVert p\rVert^{2}_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}\lVert\boldsymbol{G}_{T}(p,0)\rVert^{2}_{T}+\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(p,0)\rVert^{2}_{\partial T}.

(4.12)

Since
(\boldsymbol{G}_{T}(p,0),\boldsymbol{q})_{T}=-(p,\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{q})_{T}=0
for all
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(T;\mathbb{R}^{d})
because
\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})
, we infer that
\boldsymbol{G}_{T}(p,0)=\boldsymbol{0}
. Next, we observe that
\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(p,0)\rVert^{2}_{\partial T}=-(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(p,0),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(p|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}
owing to (
4.4
). The previous two identities in (
4.12
) result in

\displaystyle h_{T}^{-1}\lVert p\rVert^{2}_{T}\lesssim\lvert(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(p,0),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(p|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}\rvert=\lvert b_{p}(p)\rvert\leq\lVert b_{p}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}\lVert p\rVert_{T},

where the last bound follows from the definition of the
\lVert\cdot\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}
-norm. This concludes the proof.
∎

5
Proofs on the H-interpolation operator

5.1
Proof of well-posedness (Lemma
3.1
)

The linear system defining the H-interpolation operator is square in both equal- and mixed-order cases. Indeed, the number of unknowns
(d+1)\text{dim}(\mathbb{P}^{k}(T;\mathbb{R}))
(resp.
(d+1)\text{dim}(\mathbb{P}^{k}(T;\mathbb{R}))+\text{dim}(\tilde{\mathbb{P}}^{k+1}(T;\mathbb{R}))
), coincides with the number of equations,
(d+1)(\text{dim}(\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R}))+\text{dim}(\mathbb{P}^{k}(F;\mathbb{R})))
(resp.
(d+1)(\text{dim}(\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R}))+\text{dim}(\mathbb{P}^{k}(F;\mathbb{R})))+\text{dim}(\tilde{\mathbb{P}}^{k+1}(T;\mathbb{R}))
). Hence, the system is uniquely solvable if and only if we prove that
\underline{\Pi}^{\textsc{h}}_{T}(\underline{0})=\underline{0}:=(\mathbb{0},0)
. For
\underline{v}=\underline{0}
, the system (
2.17
) reduces to

\displaystyle(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),w)_{T}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall w\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R}),

(5.1a)

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}),\boldsymbol{\xi})_{T}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall\boldsymbol{\xi}\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R}^{d}),

(5.1b)

\displaystyle-(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},\mu)_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=\tau_{T}(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0)),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\mu))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\forall\mu\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R}),

(5.1c)

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}),\nabla q)_{T}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\forall q\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}(T;\mathbb{R}).

(5.1d)

Step 1
(\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T})=0)
. From (
5.1a
) with
w=\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0})
and (
5.1d
) with
q=\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})
, we have

(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}))_{T}=0=(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}),\nabla\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}))_{T}.

This with an integration by parts shows that
(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}))_{\partial T}=0
, which implies that
(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},

\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T}))_{\partial T}=0
owing to the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
. Substituting this into (
5.1c
) with
\mu:=\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T})
and using
S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0)=-S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T}))
(by (
4.4
) with
p:=\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})
) lead to

\displaystyle S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0)=0.

(5.2)

In the equal-order case, the definition of the operator
R_{T}
implies that, for all
q\in\mathbb{P}^{k+1}_{*}(T;\mathbb{R})
,

\displaystyle(\nabla R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0),\nabla q)_{T}=-(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),\Delta q)_{T}=0,

where the last identity follows from (
5.1a
) since
\Delta q\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})
. Hence,
R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0)\in\mathbb{P}^{0}(T;\mathbb{R})
so that
(1-\Pi^{k}_{T})R_{T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0)=0
. This in the definition (
2.9
) of
S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}
shows that

\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T})=S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),0)=0,

where the last equality follows from (
5.2
). In the mixed-order case, the definition (
2.9
) of
S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}
directly implies that
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T})=0
.

Step 2
(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})=0)
. Step 1 together with an integration by parts gives

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})\rVert^{2}_{T}

\displaystyle=-(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),\Delta\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}))_{T}+(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),\nabla\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=-(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}),\Delta\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}))_{T}+(\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T}),\nabla\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}=0,

where we used (
5.1a
) since
\Delta\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})
. Hence
\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})\in\mathbb{P}^{0}(T;\mathbb{R})
, but we already know that
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})|_{\partial T})=0
. So we must have
\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{0})=0
.

Step 3
(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0})=\mathbb{0})
. Recalling (
5.2
) and since
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})
, (
5.1c
) implies that
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}=0
. Since
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k-1}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0}))=\mathbb{0}
by (
5.1b
), Lemma
4.1
gives
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{0})=\mathbb{0}
.

5.2
Proof of approximation estimate (Theorem
3.2
)

(1)
Estimate on
\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v)\rVert_{T}
. Set
e^{v}:=\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v)
. The equation (
2.18a
) and the definition of
\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}
show that
e^{v}\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
. Recalling the definition (
4.11
), writing

(e^{v},0)=(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))-(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),

and using Lemma
4.6
, we infer that, for all
w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
,

\displaystyle b_{e^{v}}(w)

\displaystyle=(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\quad-(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=\tau_{T}^{-1}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma},w)_{T}-(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(w)-\phi_{v}(w),

(5.3)

with the linear forms

\displaystyle\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(w)

\displaystyle:=\tau_{T}^{-1}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma},w)_{T},

\displaystyle\phi_{v}(w)

\displaystyle:=(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})))_{\partial T}.

Since
e^{v}\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
, Lemma
4.7
, and the triangle inequality show that

\displaystyle\lVert e^{v}\rVert_{T}\lesssim h_{T}\lVert b_{e^{v}}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}\lesssim h_{T}(\lVert\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}+\lVert\phi_{v}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}).

(5.4)

Since
w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
, we have
\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(w)=\tau_{T}^{-1}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}),w)_{T}
. Recalling that
\tau_{T}^{-1}=\ell_{\Omega}^{-1}h_{T}
, this proves that

\displaystyle\lVert\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}\leq\ell_{\Omega}^{-1}h_{T}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{T}.

(5.5)

Furthermore, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, (
4.3
) from Lemma
4.4
, and Lemma
4.3
for
(0,w|_{\partial T})\in\hat{V}^{k}_{T}
show that

\displaystyle\lvert\phi_{v}(w)\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v))\rVert_{T}\lVert\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w|_{\partial T})\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v))\rVert_{T}\lVert w\rVert_{T},

where we used the
L^{2}
-stability of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
and a discrete trace inequality in the last step. This with the definition of the dual norm on
(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}
proves that

\displaystyle\lVert\phi_{v}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}\lesssim\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}_{T}^{k+1}(v))\rVert_{T}.

(5.6)

The combination of (
5.5
) and (
5.6
) in (
5.4
) proves the bound (
3.1a
).

(2)
Estimate on
\lVert\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{T}
.
We fix
d
faces
F_{1},\dots,F_{d}
of
T
. Applying Lemma
4.1
to
\boldsymbol{q}:=\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})
and using (
2.17b
) which gives
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k-1}_{T}(\boldsymbol{q})=\mathbb{0}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\in\{1:d\}}h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{F_{i}}.

The discrete Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and triangle inequality imply that

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{\partial T})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{\partial T}+\lVert(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{\partial T})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{\partial T})

\displaystyle=:T_{1}+T_{2}.

(5.7)

The
L^{2}
-stability of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
gives

\displaystyle T_{1}=h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\sigma})|_{\partial T})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{\partial T}\leq h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma})-\boldsymbol{\sigma}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert_{\partial T}.

(5.8)

Since
(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{\partial T})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})
, the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
, the definition (
2.17c
) of
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}
with
\tau_{T}=\ell_{\Omega}h_{T}^{-1}
, see (
2.8
), and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality show that

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})\setminus\{0\}}\frac{((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}|_{\partial T})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},q)_{\partial T}}{\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}}

\displaystyle=h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})\setminus\{0\}}\frac{((\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v})){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},q)_{\partial T}}{\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}}

\displaystyle=h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{q\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})\setminus\{0\}}\frac{\tau_{T}(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,q))_{\partial T}}{\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{q\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})\setminus\{0\}}\frac{\ell_{\Omega}h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,q)\rVert_{\partial T}}{\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}}.

(5.9)

In the equal-order case, the definition (
2.9
) of
S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}
implies that, for all
(0,q)\in\hat{V}_{T}^{k}
,

\displaystyle\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{eo}}(0,q)\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}+\lVert(1-\Pi^{k}_{T})R_{T}(0,q)\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}+h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(1-\Pi^{k}_{T})R_{T}(0,q)\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}+h_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla R_{T}(0,q)\rVert\lesssim\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T},

using the triangle inequality, a discrete trace inequality, the Poincaré inequality in
T
, and the fact that
\lVert\nabla R_{T}(0,q)\rVert_{T}\lesssim h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}
owing to the definition of
R_{T}
.

In the mixed-order case, the definition (
2.9
) of
S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}
directly implies that
\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\rm{mo}}(0,q)\rVert_{\partial T}=\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}
. Hence, altogether, we have
\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,q)\rVert_{\partial T}\lesssim\lVert q\rVert_{\partial T}
. This in (
5.9
) gives

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\ell_{\Omega}h_{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}),\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v|_{\partial T}))\rVert_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\ell_{\Omega}h_{T}^{-1}\lVert\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v})-\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{T}(v)\rVert_{T}+\ell_{\Omega}\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}^{k+1}_{T}(v))\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{T}+\ell_{\Omega}\lVert\nabla(v-{\cal{E}}^{k+1}_{T}(v))\rVert_{T},

(5.10)

where we used Lemma
4.5
and (
3.1a
). Using the estimates (
5.8
) and (
5.10
) in (
5.7
) concludes the proof of (
3.1b
).

5.3
Proof of approximation estimate with relaxed regularity (Theorem
3.7
)

The weaker regularity assumption on
\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}
only affects the linear form
\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}
in (
5.3
) which is now defined as
\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(w):=\tau_{T}^{-1}\langle\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma},w\rangle_{T}
for all
w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
. Step (1) in the proof of Theorem
3.2
is then modified as follows. Take
w\in\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp}(T;\mathbb{R})
. Since
\langle\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}),w\rangle_{T}=(\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}),w)_{T}=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle\ell_{\Omega}|\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(w)|=h_{T}|\langle\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma},w\rangle_{T}|

\displaystyle=h_{T}|\langle\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}),w\rangle_{T}|.

The definition (
3.3
) of the dual norm
\lVert\cdot\rVert_{H^{s-1}(T)}
and an inverse estimate for
w
show that

\displaystyle\ell_{\Omega}|\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(w)|

\displaystyle\leq h_{T}\|\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\|_{H^{s-1}(T)}|w|_{H^{1-s}(T)}

\displaystyle\lesssim h_{T}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{H^{s-1}(T)}h_{T}^{s-1}\lVert w\rVert_{T}

\displaystyle=h_{T}^{s}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{H^{s-1}(T)}\lVert w\rVert_{T}.

This and the definition of the
(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}
-norm imply that

\displaystyle\lVert\phi_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert_{(\mathbb{P}^{k^{\prime}}_{\perp})^{\prime}}\lesssim\ell_{\Omega}h_{T}^{s}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi^{k-1}_{T}(\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{H^{s-1}(T)}.

(5.11)

The rest of the proof of Theorem
3.2
remaining unchanged, this readily leads to the estimate (
3.4
).

6
Proofs of error equations and error estimates

6.1
Error equations

6.1.1
Proof of Lemma
3.8

(1)

Proof of (
3.5a
)
. The discrete problem (
2.6a
) and the definition (
2.7
) of
\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}
lead, for all
t\in\overline{J}
and all
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}}\in\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k}
, to

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\textsc{h}}(t)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+(\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{F}}^{k}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\Big\{-(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}(t)),\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T})_{T}+(\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(v(t)|_{\partial T}),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T}{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}\Big\}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T})_{\Omega}+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\big\{-(v(t),\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T})_{T}+(v(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T}{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}\big\}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+(\nabla v(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T})_{\Omega}=(\partial_{t}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t))),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega},

where we used the definition of
\Pi^{v}_{T}
, (
2.17a
), and the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
on the fourth line (observe that
\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T}\in\mathbb{P}^{k-1}(T;\mathbb{R})
and
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{T}{\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\in\mathbb{P}^{k}(\mathcal{F}_{\partial T};\mathbb{R})
), an integration by parts and the model problem (
2.1a
) on the fifth line. This proves (
3.5a
).

(2)

Proof of (
3.5b
)
. The discrete problem (
2.6b
) and the continuous problem (
2.1b
) show that, for all
t\in\overline{J}
and all
\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}\in\hat{V}_{\mathcal{M}0}^{k}
,

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}e^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}))_{\Omega}+s_{\mathcal{M}}(\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}v(t)-\nabla{\cdot}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\qquad-s_{\mathcal{M}}((\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{F}}^{k}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}(v(t)-\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t))),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\Big\{(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\nabla w_{T})_{T}-(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{T})_{\partial T}-(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}(t)),\nabla w_{T})_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}(t)){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{T}-w_{\partial T})_{\partial T}\Big\}-s_{\mathcal{M}}((\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{F}}^{k}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}),

where we used an integration by parts (recall that
\boldsymbol{\sigma}\in\boldsymbol{H}^{\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}(\Omega),\nu_{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}>\frac{1}{2}
by assumption), and the definition (
2.7
) of
\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}
on the third line. Noticing that

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\nabla w_{T})_{T}-(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{T})_{\partial T}-(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}(t)),\nabla w_{T})_{T}=-(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)|_{\partial T}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{T})_{\partial T}

owing to (
2.17d
), we arrive at

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}e^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}))_{\Omega}+s_{\mathcal{M}}(\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}(v(t)-\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t))),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\Big\{((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}(t))-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)|_{\partial T}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{T}-w_{\partial T})_{\partial T}\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad-s_{\mathcal{M}}((\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{F}}^{k}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}),

(6.1)

where we used that
\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)|_{\partial T}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{\partial T})_{\partial T}=0
. Invoking the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}
, using (
2.17c
) with
\mu:=\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w_{T}|_{\partial T})-w_{\partial T}
, and observing that
S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\hat{w}_{T})=-S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w_{T}|_{\partial T})-w_{\partial T}))_{\partial T}
owing to (
4.4
), we infer that

\displaystyle\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\Big\{((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}(t))-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\partial T}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)|_{\partial T}){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},w_{T}-w_{\partial T})_{\partial T}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}((\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{T}(\underline{v}(t))-\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w_{T}|_{\partial T})-w_{\partial T})_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\tau_{T}(S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\Pi^{v}_{T}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\partial T}^{k}(v(t)|_{\partial T})),S_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0,\Pi^{k}_{\partial T}(w_{T}|_{\partial T})-w_{\partial T}))_{\partial T}

\displaystyle\quad=s_{\mathcal{M}}((\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{F}}^{k}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}).

This in (
6.1
) concludes the proof of (
3.5b
).

6.1.2
Proof of Lemma
3.9

One can refer to
[
2
, Theorem 3]
.

6.1.3
Proof of Corollary
3.10

Shifting the terms on the right-hand side of (
3.5
) to the left-hand side gives

\displaystyle\Big\{(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(\underline{v}(t))),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}\Big\}-(\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\textsc{h}}(t)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}=0,

\displaystyle\Big\{(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}(v(t)-\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t))),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}\Big\}+(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}))_{\Omega}+s_{\mathcal{M}}(\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}})=0.

The
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}
and
\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{\mathcal{T}}
lead to

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(\underline{v}(t))),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega},

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}(v(t)-\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega},

and, consequently, prove (
3.8
).

6.1.4
Proof of Corollary
3.11

From the error equation (
3.5b
) at
t=0
and the initial conditions
\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(0)=\boldsymbol{0}
and
e^{\textsc{h}}_{\mathcal{T}}(0)=0
, we infer that

s_{\mathcal{M}}((0,e_{\mathcal{F}}^{\textsc{h}}(0)),(0,w_{\mathcal{F}}))=0\qquad\forall w_{\mathcal{F}}\in V_{\mathcal{F}0}^{k}.

This implies that
e_{\mathcal{F}}^{\textsc{h}}(0)=0
. Invoking these initial conditions in (
3.8a
)-(
3.8b
) at
t=0
proves the claim.

6.2
The energy argument (Proof of Theorem
3.14
)

For all
t\in\overline{J}
, recall the space semi-discrete errors

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(t)

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\mathcal{T}}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\qquad

\displaystyle\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\textsc{h}}(t)

\displaystyle:=\hat{v}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)-(\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{F}}^{k}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\mathcal{T}}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\qquad

\displaystyle\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)

\displaystyle:=\hat{v}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)-(\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{\mathcal{T}}(v(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})).

(1)
As shown in
[
2
]
, substituting
\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}:=\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)
in (
3.6b
) and
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}}:=\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)
in (
3.6a
) for all
t\in\widetilde{J}
, we obtain after some straightforward manipulations

\displaystyle\lVert e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rVert^{2}_{C^{0}(J_{t};\Omega)}+\lVert\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rVert^{2}_{C^{0}(J_{t};\Omega)}+|\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}|^{2}_{L^{2}(J_{t};\textsc{s})}\lesssim\lVert e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0)\rVert^{2}_{\Omega}+\lVert\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0)\rVert^{2}_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+\lVert\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\underline{v};\cdot)\rVert^{2}_{L^{\infty}(J_{t};(\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}})^{\prime})}+\lVert\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\partial_{t}\underline{v};\cdot)\rVert^{2}_{L^{1}(J_{t};(\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}})^{\prime})}.

The initial condition (
3.12
) and the Pythagoras identity based on the
L^{2}
-orthogonalities of
\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}
and
\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k}
imply that

\displaystyle\lVert e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0)\rVert_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=\lVert\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{v}(\underline{v}_{0})-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(v_{0})\rVert_{\Omega}\leq\lVert v_{0}-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{v}(\underline{v}_{0})\rVert_{\Omega},

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0)\rVert_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=\lVert\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(\underline{v}_{0})-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k}(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{0})\rVert_{\Omega}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}(\underline{v}_{0})\rVert_{\Omega}.

Inserting these inequalities in the first inequality of this step and taking the square root proves (
3.13
).

(2)
Differentiating in time the error equations, we can similarly obtain

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}\rVert^{2}_{C^{0}(J_{t};\Omega)}+\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}\rVert^{2}_{C^{0}(J_{t};\Omega)}+|\partial_{t}\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}|^{2}_{L^{2}(J_{t};\textsc{s})}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{t}e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(0)\rVert^{2}_{\Omega}+\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(0)\rVert^{2}_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+\lVert\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\partial_{t}\underline{v};\cdot)\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J_{t};(\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}})^{\prime})}^{2}+\lVert\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\partial_{tt}\underline{v};\cdot)\rVert_{L^{1}(J_{t};(\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}})^{\prime})}^{2}.

This followed by the zero initial conditions (
3.9
) concludes the proof of (
3.14
).

6.3
The duality argument (Proof of Theorem
3.16
)

Step 1 (Dual problems)
Following
[
8
]
, we introduce a dual problem and a time-integrated dual problem. However, we only assume that the elliptic regularity pickup satisfies
s\in(\frac{1}{2},1]
instead of
s=1
in
[
8
]
. Let
\theta\in L^{2}(\Omega)
, and let
\Phi_{\theta}
be the solution of the homogeneous wave equation

\displaystyle\partial_{tt}\Phi_{\theta}-\Delta\Phi_{\theta}=0\qquad\text{in}\;J\times\Omega,

(6.2)

with the
final
and boundary conditions

\displaystyle\Phi_{\theta}(T_{f})=0,\;\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta}(T_{f})=\theta\qquad

\displaystyle\text{on}\;\Omega,

(6.3a)

\displaystyle\Phi_{\theta}(t,x)=0\qquad

\displaystyle\text{on}\;J\times\Gamma.

(6.3b)

The following regularity result is classical (see, e.g.,
[
1
, Chapter 8]
):

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla\Phi_{\theta}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}+\lVert\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))}\lesssim\lVert\theta\rVert_{\Omega}.

(6.4)

We set
\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t):=\int_{t}^{T_{f}}\Phi_{\theta}(s)\,\mathrm{ds}
for all
t\in\overline{J}
. A straightforward calculation shows that
\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}
satisfies the inhomogenous wave equation

\displaystyle\partial_{tt}\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}-\Delta\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}=-\theta\quad\text{in}\;J\times\Omega,

(6.5)

with the final and boundary conditions

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(T_{f})=0,\;\partial_{t}\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(T_{f})=0\quad

\displaystyle\text{on}\;\Omega,

(6.6a)

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t,x)=0\quad

\displaystyle\text{on}\;J\times\Gamma.

(6.6b)

Moreover, invoking elliptic regularity in
\Omega
, we infer that there exists some
s\in(\frac{1}{2},1]
such that

\displaystyle\lVert\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))}+\ell_{\Omega}\lVert\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}+\,\ell_{\Omega}^{1+s}\lvert\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}\rvert_{L^{\infty}(J;H^{1+s}(\Omega))}\lesssim\ell_{\Omega}^{2}\lVert\theta\rVert_{\Omega}.

(6.7)

Step 2 (Key identity)
. Let
(\theta_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\subset(C_{0}^{\infty}(\Omega))^{\mathbb{N}}
be a sequence such that
