\theta_{n}\to\theta
in
L^{2}(\Omega)
. Without loss of generality, we assume that
\lVert\theta_{n}\rVert_{\Omega}\lesssim 2\lVert\theta\rVert_{\Omega}
for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Observe that
\Phi_{\theta_{n}}
is smooth in space and in time. The final boundary condition
\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(T_{f})=\theta_{n}
and
\utilde{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(0)=0
lead to

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}(\Phi_{\theta_{n}}):=(\utilde{e}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(T_{f}),\theta_{n})_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=(\utilde{e}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(T_{f}),\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(T_{f}))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\frac{d}{dt}(\utilde{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(t))_{\Omega}\;dt

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\left\{(e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(t))_{\Omega}+(\Delta\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(t),\utilde{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{\Omega}\right\}\,dt,

(6.8)

where we used
\partial_{t}\utilde{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t)=e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)
and
\partial_{tt}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}=\Delta\Phi_{\theta_{n}}
in the last step. For all
f\in H^{1}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))
and all
g\in L^{2}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))
with
\widetilde{g}(t):=\int_{t}^{T_{f}}g(s)\,\mathrm{ds}
, the following identity holds:

\displaystyle\int_{J}(f(t),g(t))_{\Omega}\,dt=(f(0),\widetilde{g}(0))_{\Omega}+\int_{J}(\partial_{t}f(t),\widetilde{g}(t))_{\Omega}\,dt.

(6.9)

The identity (
6.9
) for
f:=\utilde{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}
(so that
f(0)=0
and
\partial_{t}f=e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}
) and
g:=\Delta\Phi_{\theta_{n}}
implies that

\displaystyle\int_{J}(\Delta\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(t),\utilde{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{\Omega}\,dt

\displaystyle=\int_{J}(\Delta\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}(t),e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{\Omega}\,dt

\displaystyle=-\int_{J}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\Big\{(\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}(t),\nabla e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{T}-(\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}(t){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{\partial T}\Big\}dt,

(6.10)

where we used cellwise integration by parts in space on the second line.
This in (
6.8
) leads to

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}(\Phi_{\theta_{n}})

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\Big\{(e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}(t))_{\Omega}-\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\big\{(\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}(t),\nabla e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{T}-(\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}(t){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{\partial T}\big\}dt\Big\}.

We now pass to the limit
n\to\infty
in the above identity.
From (
6.4
), (
6.7
), and the linearity of the wave equation, we have
\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}\to\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta}
in
L^{\infty}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))
and
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}\to\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}
in
L^{\infty}(J;H^{1+s}(\Omega))
.
Since we have that

\lVert\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta_{n}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))}+\ell_{\Omega}^{-1}\lVert\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}+\ell_{\Omega}^{-2}\lVert\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta_{n}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(J;H^{1+s}(\Omega))}\lesssim\lVert\theta_{n}\rVert_{\Omega}\lesssim 2\lVert\theta\rVert_{\Omega},

for all
n\mathbb{N}
,
we can invoke Lebesgue’s dominated convergence theorem and infer that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}(\Phi_{\theta})

\displaystyle:=(\utilde{e}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(T_{f}),\theta)_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\Big\{(e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}-\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\big\{(\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t),\nabla e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{T}-(\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t))_{\partial T}\big\}dt\Big\}

\displaystyle=:T_{1}+T_{2}.

(6.11)

On the one hand, invoking (
6.9
) with
f:=e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\in H^{1}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))
and
g:=\partial_{t}\Phi_{\theta}\in L^{2}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))
and since
\widetilde{g}(t)=\Phi_{\theta}(T_{f})-\Phi_{\theta}(t)=-\Phi_{\theta}(t)
owing to (
6.3a
), we infer that

\displaystyle T_{1}=-(e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0),\Phi_{\theta}(0))_{\Omega}-\int_{J}(\partial_{t}e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{T}}(t),\Phi_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}\,dt.

(6.12)

On the other hand, the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}
leads to

\displaystyle T_{2}=-\int_{J}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\Big\{(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\nabla e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{T}(t))_{T}-(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T},e^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{T}(t))_{\partial T}\Big\}\,dt.

(6.13)

The definition of
\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}
in the first error equation (
3.6a
) implies that, for all
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}}\in\mathbf{\Sigma}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}
,

\displaystyle-(\nabla e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}

(6.14)

Differentiating in time the second error equation (

), we obtain, for all

,

\displaystyle-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}))_{\Omega}=(\partial_{tt}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),w_{\mathcal{T}})_{\Omega}+s_{\mathcal{M}}(\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}})-\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\ra This with

in (

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\Big\{(\partial_{tt}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}+s_{\mathcal{M}}(\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})))

\displaystyle\quad-\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\partial_{t}\underline{v}(t);(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})))

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}(e_{T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)-e_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}\Big\}\,dt.

(6.15)

Since
\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(T_{f})=0
,
\partial_{t}\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)=-\Phi_{\theta}(t)
, and
\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0)=0
owing to (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{J}(\partial_{tt}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}\,dt

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\Big\{\frac{d}{dt}(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}\Big\}\,dt

\displaystyle=\int_{J}(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Phi_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}\,dt.

This with the
L^{2}
-orthogonality of
\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{\mathcal{T}}
in (
6.15
) shows that

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{J}\Big\{(\partial_{t}e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),\Phi_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}\,dt+s_{\mathcal{M}}(\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})))

\displaystyle\quad-\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\partial_{t}\underline{v}(t);(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})))

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}(e_{T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)-e_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}\Big\}\,dt.

(6.16)

The combination of (
6.12
) and (
6.16
) in (
6.11
) proves that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}(\Phi_{\theta})

\displaystyle=-(e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0),\Phi_{\theta}(0))_{\Omega}+\int_{J}\Big\{s_{\mathcal{M}}(\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})))

\displaystyle\quad-\psi_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(\partial_{t}\underline{v}(t);(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})))

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}(e_{T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)-e_{\partial T}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t),(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T})_{\partial T}\Big\}\,dt

\displaystyle=:\mathfrak{E}_{0}(\Phi_{\theta}(0))+\int_{J}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathfrak{E}_{i}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))\,dt.

(6.17)

Step 3 (Bounds on
\{\mathfrak{E}_{j}\}_{j=0}^{3}
)
. For
\mathfrak{E}_{0}
, the observation
e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(0)=e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(0)+\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}_{0})-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(v_{0})=\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}_{0})-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(v_{0})
owing to the initial condition
e_{\mathcal{T}}^{\textsc{h}}(0)=0
and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality show that

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}_{0}(\Phi_{\theta}(0))|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(v_{0})-\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}_{0})\rVert_{\Omega}\lVert\Phi_{\theta}(0)\rVert_{\Omega}.

(6.18)

For
\mathfrak{E}_{1}
, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality followed by (
4.3
) and the classical approximation properties for the elliptic projection
{\cal E}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k+1}
give

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}_{1}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))|

\displaystyle\leq|\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)|_{\textsc{s}}|(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}}))|_{\textsc{s}}\lesssim\ell_{\Omega}^{\frac{1}{2}}h^{s}|\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)|_{\textsc{s}}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s}(\Omega)}.

(6.19)

For
\mathfrak{E}_{2}
, substituting
\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}}:=(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}}))
in (
3.7
) and since
\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{w}_{\mathcal{M}})=\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))
, we infer that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{2}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))

\displaystyle=(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}+(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\quad+s_{\mathcal{M}}((\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}v(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\partial_{t}v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}}))).

(6.20)

The
L^{2}
-orthogonality of the projection operators and the fact that
(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}=-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}
owing to the boundary condition
\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)=0
on
\Gamma
show that

\displaystyle(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}+(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))-\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}+(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}+(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t),\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))-\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}.

This in the identity (
6.20
) proves that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{2}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))

\displaystyle=(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))-\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\quad-(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)),\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)))_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\quad+s_{\mathcal{M}}((\Pi^{k^{\prime}}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}v(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\partial_{t}v(t)|_{\mathcal{F}})),(\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)),\Pi^{k}_{\mathcal{F}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{\mathcal{F}}))).

(6.21)

Invoking the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, the approximation estimate (
2.3
) for the projection operator
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}
, and bounding the stabilization term as above leads to

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}_{2}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))|\lesssim{}

\displaystyle h^{s}\Big\{\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t))\rVert_{\Omega}+\ell_{\Omega}\lVert\nabla(\partial_{t}v-{\cal E}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k+1}(\partial_{t}v))\rVert_{\Omega}\Big\}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle+\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t))\rVert_{\Omega}\lVert\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))-\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)\rVert_{\Omega}.

The bound on the last term on the right-hand side depends on whether
k^{\prime}=0
or
k^{\prime}\geq 1
.
Recalling that
\delta:=s
if
k^{\prime}=0
and
\delta:=0
otherwise, we obtain

\lVert\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))-\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)\rVert_{\Omega}\lesssim h^{1+s-\delta}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s-\delta}(\Omega)}.

Altogether, this gives

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}_{2}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))|\lesssim{}

\displaystyle h^{s}\Big\{\big\{\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t))\rVert_{\Omega}+\ell_{\Omega}\lVert\nabla(\partial_{t}v-{\cal E}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k+1}(\partial_{t}v))\rVert_{\Omega}\big\}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle+h^{1-\delta}\ell_{\Omega}^{\delta}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t)-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}(t))\rVert_{\Omega}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s-\delta}(\Omega)}\Big\}.

For
\mathfrak{E}_{3}
, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, the fractional multiplicative trace inequality (see
[
18
, Remark 12.19]
), and the approximation property (
2.3
) for
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}
lead to

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}_{3}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)\rVert_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\Big\{\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}h_{T}\lVert\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{T}(\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))){\cdot}\boldsymbol{n}_{T}\rVert^{2}_{\partial T}\Big\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim h^{s}\lVert\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)\rVert_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s}(\Omega)}.

(6.22)

Notice from (
3.6a
) that
\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathcal{T}}(\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}(t))=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)
for all
t\in\overline{J}
. This in
Lemma
4.3
implies that

\displaystyle\lVert\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)\rVert_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)\rVert_{\Omega}+\ell_{\Omega}^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)|_{\textsc{s}}.

(6.23)

This in (
6.22
) proves that

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}_{3}(\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t))|\lesssim h^{s}\Big\{\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(t)\rVert_{\Omega}+\ell_{\Omega}^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\hat{e}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}_{\mathcal{M}}(t)|_{\textsc{s}}\Big\}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}(t)|_{H^{1+s}(\Omega)}.

(6.24)

Step 4 (Conclusion)
. Collecting the previous estimates, invoking the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality in time, and using that
|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}|_{L^{2}(J;H^{1+s}(\Omega))}\leq T_{f}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}|_{L^{\infty}(J;H^{1+s}(\Omega))}
proves that

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{E}(\Phi_{\theta})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(v_{0})-\Pi^{v}_{\mathcal{T}}(\underline{v}_{0})\rVert_{\Omega}\|\Phi_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))}

\displaystyle+h^{s}T_{f}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\{\big\{\ell_{\Omega}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lvert\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rvert_{L^{2}(J;\textsc{s})}+\ell_{\Omega}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lvert\partial_{t}\hat{e}_{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rvert_{L^{2}(J;\textsc{s})}+\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}\rVert_{L^{2}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}

\displaystyle+\ell_{\Omega}\lVert\nabla(\partial_{t}v-{\cal E}_{\mathcal{T}}^{k+1}(\partial_{t}v))\rVert_{L^{2}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}+\lVert\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{k}_{\mathcal{T}}(\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{L^{2}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}\big\}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}|_{L^{\infty}(J;H^{1+s-\delta}(\Omega))}

\displaystyle+h^{1-\delta}\ell_{\Omega}^{\delta}\lVert\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma}-\Pi_{\mathcal{T}}^{k^{\prime}}(\nabla{\cdot}\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\sigma})\rVert_{L^{2}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))}|\tilde{\Phi}_{\theta}|_{L^{\infty}(J;H^{1+s}(\Omega))}\Big\}.

Since
\|\Phi_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}(J;L^{2}(\Omega))}\lesssim\ell_{\Omega}\|\nabla\Phi_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}(J;\boldsymbol{L}^{2}(\Omega))}
owing to the Poincaré inequality in
\Omega
,
applying the regularity estimates (
6.4
) and (
6.7
) and choosing
\theta:=\utilde{e}_{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1229281}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{1092269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\textsc{HHO}\mathstrut$}}}}}}(T_{f})
concludes the proof.
